# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title flights Model

class Airline {
    flights : List<Flight>
    addFlight(f : Flight) : void
    closeFlight(flightId : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    publishFlight(f : Flight, now : EDate) : boolean
    removeFlight(f : Flight) : void
    searchFlights(origin : String, dest : String, date : EDate) : List<Flight>
}
class Airport {
    id : String
    servesForCities : List<City>
    addCity(c : City) : void
}
class Booking {
    customer : Customer
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    createReservation(f : Flight, passenger : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class City {
}
class Customer {
    bookings : List<Booking>
    addBooking(f : Flight, now : EDate, listOfPassengerNames : List<String>) : boolean
    cancel(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
    confirm(reservationID : String, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Flight {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    id : String
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a newly created Flight object to the Airline's collection of flights so that the airline can subsequently manage it (e.g., publish or close).
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return without action.
  2. If f.id already exists in flights, return without action (avoid duplicates).
  3. Otherwise, append f to the flights list.
Related Features: flights (the list of Flight objects owned by the Airline)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f (Flight) — a newly created flight instance to be associated with the airline. Its id must be unique within the airline.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight with a unique id that is not already present in this Airline's flights list.
Post-condition: The given Flight f is stored in this Airline's flights list, making it available for further operations such as publishFlight/closeFlight.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an existing open flight that has not yet departed, marking it as no longer bookable and canceling all of its currently CONFIRMED reservations.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the Flight in flights whose id equals flightId; if not found, return false.
  2. If flight.openForBooking == false, return false (only an open flight can be closed).
  3. Compute the calendar day of now and the calendar day of flight.departureTime.
  4. If these two calendar days are equal, return false (closing is forbidden on the same calendar day as departure).
  5. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false (the flight has already departed).
  6. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  7. For each reservation r in flight.reservations, if r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - flightId (String) — the unique id of the target flight.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used for temporal checks.
Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully closed and all confirmed reservations were canceled; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A flight with the given flightId exists; now is the current timestamp; the flight is currently openForBooking; the current calendar day differs from the flight's departure calendar day.
Post-condition: flight.openForBooking becomes false; every previously CONFIRMED reservation of that flight transitions to CANCELED; PENDING reservations are left untouched.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Searches the Airline's flights for those that depart from a given origin airport, arrive at a given destination airport, and depart on a given date.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty result list.
  2. For each Flight f in flights:
       - if f.departureAirport != null and f.departureAirport.id == origin,
         and f.arrivalAirport != null and f.arrivalAirport.id == dest,
         and the date portion of f.departureTime equals the date portion of date,
         then add f to result.
  3. Return result (empty list if no matches).
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight, Airport
Input:
  - origin (String) — the id of the desired departure Airport.
  - dest (String) — the id of the desired arrival Airport.
  - date (EDate) — the desired departure date; the time portion may be ignored when matching.
Output: List<Flight> — the list of matching flights; may be empty.
Pre-condition: None. The search is read-only and does not modify the airline's state.
Post-condition: A filtered list of flights is returned; the airline's flights list is unchanged.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a Flight from the Airline's collection of flights (typically used for administrative cleanup or after a flight has been added in error).
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return without action.
  2. Remove f from the flights list if present.
  3. Return without action otherwise.
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f (Flight) — the flight to be removed from the airline's catalog.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight reference; it may or may not currently exist in the airline's flights list.
Post-condition: If f was in flights, it is no longer present; otherwise the list is unchanged.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a newly created Flight to the public booking catalog after validating its timestamps, temporal ordering, and route integrity.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return false.
  2. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are parseable timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format; if not, return false.
  3. If f.departureAirport == null or f.arrivalAirport == null, return false.
  4. If f.departureAirport.id == f.arrivalAirport.id, return false (route integrity).
  5. If !(now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime), return false (temporal consistency).
  6. If f.openForBooking == true, return false (a flight can only be published once).
  7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  8. If f is not yet in flights, add it to flights.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight, Airport
Input:
  - f (Flight) — the flight to be published; contains id, departure/arrival Airports, departureTime, arrivalTime, and openForBooking status.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used as the lower bound for departure time.
Output: boolean — true if the flight is successfully published; false if any validation fails or it has already been published.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null, freshly created Flight with valid timestamps, distinct departure and arrival airports, f.openForBooking == false, and now precedes f.departureTime.
Post-condition: f.openForBooking becomes true; the flight is included in this Airline's flights list and becomes available for customer bookings.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Class Diagram

# Operation Specifications
Airline::addFlight
Summary: Adds a newly created Flight object to the Airline's collection of flights so that the airline can subsequently manage it (e.g., publish or close).
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return without action.
  2. If f.id already exists in flights, return without action (avoid duplicates).
  3. Otherwise, append f to the flights list.
Related Features: flights (the list of Flight objects owned by the Airline)
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f (Flight) — a newly created flight instance to be associated with the airline. Its id must be unique within the airline.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight with a unique id that is not already present in this Airline's flights list.
Post-condition: The given Flight f is stored in this Airline's flights list, making it available for further operations such as publishFlight/closeFlight.

Airline::closeFlight
Summary: Closes an existing open flight that has not yet departed, marking it as no longer bookable and canceling all of its currently CONFIRMED reservations.
Algorithm:
  1. Find the Flight in flights whose id equals flightId; if not found, return false.
  2. If flight.openForBooking == false, return false (only an open flight can be closed).
  3. Compute the calendar day of now and the calendar day of flight.departureTime.
  4. If these two calendar days are equal, return false (closing is forbidden on the same calendar day as departure).
  5. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false (the flight has already departed).
  6. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
  7. For each reservation r in flight.reservations, if r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - flightId (String) — the unique id of the target flight.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used for temporal checks.
Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully closed and all confirmed reservations were canceled; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A flight with the given flightId exists; now is the current timestamp; the flight is currently openForBooking; the current calendar day differs from the flight's departure calendar day.
Post-condition: flight.openForBooking becomes false; every previously CONFIRMED reservation of that flight transitions to CANCELED; PENDING reservations are left untouched.

Airline::searchFlights
Summary: Searches the Airline's flights for those that depart from a given origin airport, arrive at a given destination airport, and depart on a given date.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize an empty result list.
  2. For each Flight f in flights:
       - if f.departureAirport != null and f.departureAirport.id == origin,
         and f.arrivalAirport != null and f.arrivalAirport.id == dest,
         and the date portion of f.departureTime equals the date portion of date,
         then add f to result.
  3. Return result (empty list if no matches).
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight, Airport
Input:
  - origin (String) — the id of the desired departure Airport.
  - dest (String) — the id of the desired arrival Airport.
  - date (EDate) — the desired departure date; the time portion may be ignored when matching.
Output: List<Flight> — the list of matching flights; may be empty.
Pre-condition: None. The search is read-only and does not modify the airline's state.
Post-condition: A filtered list of flights is returned; the airline's flights list is unchanged.

Airline::removeFlight
Summary: Removes a Flight from the Airline's collection of flights (typically used for administrative cleanup or after a flight has been added in error).
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return without action.
  2. Remove f from the flights list if present.
  3. Return without action otherwise.
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight
Input: f (Flight) — the flight to be removed from the airline's catalog.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight reference; it may or may not currently exist in the airline's flights list.
Post-condition: If f was in flights, it is no longer present; otherwise the list is unchanged.

Airline::publishFlight
Summary: Publishes a newly created Flight to the public booking catalog after validating its timestamps, temporal ordering, and route integrity.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null, return false.
  2. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are parseable timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format; if not, return false.
  3. If f.departureAirport == null or f.arrivalAirport == null, return false.
  4. If f.departureAirport.id == f.arrivalAirport.id, return false (route integrity).
  5. If !(now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime), return false (temporal consistency).
  6. If f.openForBooking == true, return false (a flight can only be published once).
  7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
  8. If f is not yet in flights, add it to flights.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: flights
Related Classes: Flight, Airport
Input:
  - f (Flight) — the flight to be published; contains id, departure/arrival Airports, departureTime, arrivalTime, and openForBooking status.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used as the lower bound for departure time.
Output: boolean — true if the flight is successfully published; false if any validation fails or it has already been published.
Pre-condition: f is a non-null, freshly created Flight with valid timestamps, distinct departure and arrival airports, f.openForBooking == false, and now precedes f.departureTime.
Post-condition: f.openForBooking becomes true; the flight is included in this Airline's flights list and becomes available for customer bookings.

# Operation Specifications
Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a new Booking for this Customer covering a list of passengers on a specific open flight, generating one PENDING Reservation per passenger.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null or listOfPassengerNames is null or empty, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  3. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  4. Check that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicates (case-insensitive, trimmed); if duplicates exist, return false.
  5. Create a new Booking b and set b.customer = this.
  6. For each name in listOfPassengerNames:
       - Call b.createReservation(f, name, now).
       - If it returns false, abandon the booking (roll back) and return false.
  7. Append b to this Customer's bookings.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings
Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - f (Flight) — the open flight to be booked.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
  - listOfPassengerNames (List<String>) — the non-empty list of passenger names to be reserved on the flight; must contain no duplicates.
Output: boolean — true if a booking was created and all reservations were added successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is an open, not-yet-departed flight; listOfPassengerNames is non-empty and contains no duplicate names; now precedes f.departureTime.
Post-condition: A new Booking is created and added to this Customer's bookings; the flight gains a new PENDING Reservation for every supplied passenger name.

Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing PENDING Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.
  2. If r.status != PENDING, return false (only PENDING reservations can be confirmed).
  3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
  4. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: bookings
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to confirm.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully confirmed; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the reservation's status is PENDING; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime.
Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CONFIRMED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.
  2. If r.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled).
  3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
  4. Set r.status = CANCELED.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: bookings
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to cancel.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully canceled; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime; the reservation is not already CANCELED.
Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CANCELED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.

```plantuml
    openForBooking : boolean
    arrivalAirport : Airport
    departureAirport : Airport
    reservations : List<Reservation>
    stopovers : List<Stopover>
    addStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
    getConfirmedReservations() : List<Reservation>
    removeStopover(stop : Stopover, now : EDate) : boolean
}
class Passenger {
    name : String
}
class Reservation {
    id : String
    status : ReservationStatus
    flight : Flight
    passenger : Passenger
}
class Stopover {
    arrivalTime : EDate
    departureTime : EDate
    airport : Airport
}
enum ReservationStatus {
    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
}

Airline *-- "*" Flight : flights
Airport --> "*" City : servesForCities
Booking --> Customer : customer
Booking *-- "*" Reservation : reservations
Customer --> "*" Booking : bookings
Flight --> Airport : arrivalAirport
Flight --> Airport : departureAirport
Flight --> "*" Reservation : reservations
Flight *-- "*" Stopover : stopovers
Reservation --> Flight : flight
Reservation --> Passenger : passenger
Stopover --> Airport : airport
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Customer::addBooking
Summary: Creates a new Booking for this Customer covering a list of passengers on a specific open flight, generating one PENDING Reservation per passenger.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null or listOfPassengerNames is null or empty, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  3. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
  4. Check that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicates (case-insensitive, trimmed); if duplicates exist, return false.
  5. Create a new Booking b and set b.customer = this.
  6. For each name in listOfPassengerNames:
       - Call b.createReservation(f, name, now).
       - If it returns false, abandon the booking (roll back) and return false.
  7. Append b to this Customer's bookings.
  8. Return true.
Related Features: bookings
Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - f (Flight) — the open flight to be booked.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
  - listOfPassengerNames (List<String>) — the non-empty list of passenger names to be reserved on the flight; must contain no duplicates.
Output: boolean — true if a booking was created and all reservations were added successfully; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is an open, not-yet-departed flight; listOfPassengerNames is non-empty and contains no duplicate names; now precedes f.departureTime.
Post-condition: A new Booking is created and added to this Customer's bookings; the flight gains a new PENDING Reservation for every supplied passenger name.

Customer::confirm
Summary: Confirms an existing PENDING Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.
  2. If r.status != PENDING, return false (only PENDING reservations can be confirmed).
  3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
  4. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: bookings
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to confirm.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully confirmed; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the reservation's status is PENDING; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime.
Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CONFIRMED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.

Customer::cancel
Summary: Cancels an existing Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.
  2. If r.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled).
  3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
  4. Set r.status = CANCELED.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: bookings
Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to cancel.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully canceled; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime; the reservation is not already CANCELED.
Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CANCELED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Booking::createReservation
Summary: Creates a new Reservation for a single passenger on a given flight, links it to this Booking and the Flight, and sets the reservation's initial status to PENDING.
Algorithm:
  1. If f is null or passenger is null/empty, return false.
  2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
  3. If now >= f.departureTime, return false (cannot reserve after departure).
  4. If f already has a reservation whose passenger.name equals passenger (case-insensitive trim), return false (no duplicate passenger on the flight).
  5. Generate a new unique id for the reservation.
  6. Create a new Passenger with name = passenger, a new Reservation with that id, status = PENDING, flight = f, passenger = the new Passenger.
  7. Append the new Reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
  8. Append the new Reservation to f.reservations.
  9. Return true.
Related Features: reservations
Related Classes: Reservation, Passenger, Flight, ReservationStatus
Input:
  - f (Flight) — the open flight the passenger is being reserved on.
  - passenger (String) — the passenger's full name; must be unique on the flight.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
Output: boolean — true if a new PENDING reservation was successfully created and linked; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: f is open for booking, now is before f.departureTime, and no existing reservation on f carries the same passenger name.
Post-condition: A new Reservation (status PENDING) is added to both this Booking's reservations and f.reservations; the flight's openForBooking status is unchanged.

```plantuml
    departureTime : EDate

    name : String

    PENDING
    CONFIRMED
    CANCELED
```

# Operation Specifications
Airport::addCity
Summary: Associates a City with the Airport, indicating that the airport serves that city.
Algorithm:
  1. If c is null, return without action.
  2. If servesForCities already contains c, return without action.
  3. Otherwise, append c to servesForCities.
Related Features: servesForCities
Related Classes: City
Input: c (City) — the city to be linked to this airport.
Output: void — no return value.
Pre-condition: c is a non-null City that is not already associated with this airport.
Post-condition: c is present in this Airport's servesForCities list.

# Requirement
```text
Airlines offer different flights. Each flight has its unique id. A flight is open for booking and closed by order of the company. A customer can book one or more flights, for different passengers. A reservation concerns a single flight and a single passenger, has a unique id. A reservation can be confirmed or canceled. A flight has a departure airport and an arrival airport. Each airport has its unique id. A flight has a day and time of departure, and a day and time of arrival (e.g. 2024-12-08 13:00:00). A flight may include stopovers in one or more airport(s). A stopover has a departure time and an arrival time. Each airport serves one or more cities.
Functional requirement:
1.Publish a flight for booking. An airline can publish a newly created flight to make it available for customer bookings. The system first confirms that the flight has valid departure and arrival timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, then checks temporal consistency — currentTime < departureTime < arrivalTime — and route integrity — departureAirport ≠ arrivalAirport. A flight may be published only once, and before publishing, the flight's openForBooking status must be false. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
2.Create a booking for passengers on a flight. A customer selects a specific open flight and supplies a list of passengers names. The system checks that there are no duplicate passengers on the flight and the current time is before the flight departure time. It then generates a booking for the customer and creates a reservation with a unique ID for each passenger in that booking. Each reservation is initially placed in "pending" status until the customer confirms it. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
3.Confirm or cancel an existing reservation in a booking. The system checks that the flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking. The customer can then confirm or cancel a reservation by providing its reservation ID. The call returns true on success; otherwise, false.
4.Close an open flight. An airline can close an existing flight that has not yet departed. The system verifies that the flight is currently open; if so, it changes the status to closed and cancels every confirmed reservation. Closing is not allowed on the same calendar day as the flight's departure — even if the current time is before the departure time. It returns true on success; otherwise, false.
5.Retrieve all confirmed reservations for a specific flight. Given a flight object, the system retrieves every confirmed reservation on that flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking. It returns a list of reservations, or empty list if none exist.
```

# Operation Specifications
Flight::getConfirmedReservations
Summary: Returns every CONFIRMED reservation on this flight, provided the flight is currently open for booking.
Algorithm:
  1. If openForBooking == false, return an empty list.
  2. Initialize an empty result list.
  3. For each reservation r in reservations:
       - if r.status == CONFIRMED, add r to result.
  4. Return result.
Related Features: reservations, openForBooking
Related Classes: Reservation, ReservationStatus
Input: (none)
Output: List<Reservation> — the list of CONFIRMED reservations on this flight, or an empty list if the flight is closed or has no confirmed reservations.
Pre-condition: The flight instance exists; openForBooking is either true (returning possibly populated list) or false (returning empty list).
Post-condition: The flight's state is unchanged; a filtered view of its reservations is returned.

Flight::removeStopover
Summary: Removes a Stopover from the Flight's list of stopovers, provided the flight has not yet departed.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. If now >= this.departureTime, return false (cannot remove stopover after the flight has departed).
  3. If stopovers does not contain stop, return false.
  4. Remove stop from stopovers.
  5. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers, departureTime
Related Classes: Stopover
Input:
  - stop (Stopover) — the stopover to remove.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used to ensure the stopover is removed before departure.
Output: boolean — true if the stopover was successfully removed; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is a non-null Stopover currently present in this flight's stopovers list; the flight has not yet departed.
Post-condition: stop is no longer in this Flight's stopovers list; the flight's other attributes remain unchanged.

Flight::addStopover
Summary: Adds a Stopover (an intermediate airport visit) to the Flight's list of stopovers, subject to basic temporal validation.
Algorithm:
  1. If stop is null, return false.
  2. If stop.airport is null, or stop.arrivalTime / stop.departureTime are not in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, return false.
  3. If !(stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime), return false.
  4. If now >= this.departureTime, return false (cannot add stopover after the flight has departed).
  5. If stopovers already contains a stopover at stop.airport, return false (avoid duplicate airport stopover).
  6. Append stop to stopovers.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers, departureTime
Related Classes: Stopover, Airport
Input:
  - stop (Stopover) — the stopover to add, with airport, arrivalTime, and departureTime.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used to ensure the stopover is added before departure.
Output: boolean — true if the stopover was successfully added; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is a non-null Stopover with a valid airport and consistent, parseable arrival/departure times; the flight has not yet departed; no stopover for the same airport already exists on this flight.
Post-condition: stop is included in this Flight's stopovers list; the flight's other attributes remain unchanged.

  3. If !(stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime), return false.
  4. If now >= this.departureTime, return false (cannot add stopover after the flight has departed).
  5. If stopovers already contains a stopover at stop.airport, return false (avoid duplicate airport stopover).
  6. Append stop to stopovers.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: stopovers, departureTime
Related Classes: Stopover, Airport
Input:
  - stop (Stopover) — the stopover to add, with airport, arrivalTime, and departureTime.
  - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used to ensure the stopover is added before departure.
Output: boolean — true if the stopover was successfully added; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: stop is a non-null Stopover with a valid airport and consistent, parseable arrival/departure times; the flight has not yet departed; no stopover for the same airport already exists on this flight.
Post-condition: stop is included in this Flight's stopovers list; the flight's other attributes remain unchanged.

   * Summary: Adds a Stopover (an intermediate airport visit) to the Flight's list of stopovers, subject to basic temporal validation.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If stop is null, return false.
   *   2. If stop.airport is null, or stop.arrivalTime / stop.departureTime are not in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, return false.
   *   3. If !(stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime), return false.
   *   4. If now >= this.departureTime, return false (cannot add stopover after the flight has departed).
   *   5. If stopovers already contains a stopover at stop.airport, return false (avoid duplicate airport stopover).
   *   6. Append stop to stopovers.
   *   7. Return true.
   * Related Features: stopovers, departureTime
   * Related Classes: Stopover, Airport
   * Input:
   *   - stop (Stopover) — the stopover to add, with airport, arrivalTime, and departureTime.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used to ensure the stopover is added before departure.
   * Output: boolean — true if the stopover was successfully added; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: stop is a non-null Stopover with a valid airport and consistent, parseable arrival/departure times; the flight has not yet departed; no stopover for the same airport already exists on this flight.
   * Post-condition: stop is included in this Flight's stopovers list; the flight's other attributes remain unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addStopover(final Stopover stop, final Date now)
  {
    // 1. If stop is null, return false.
    if (stop == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 2. If stop.airport is null, or stop.arrivalTime / stop.departureTime are not in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, return false.
    if (stop.getAirport() == null || stop.getArrivalTime() == null || stop.getDepartureTime() == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    sdf.setLenient(false);
    try
    {
      String arrivalStr = sdf.format(stop.getArrivalTime());
      String departureStr = sdf.format(stop.getDepartureTime());
      sdf.parse(arrivalStr);
      sdf.parse(departureStr);
    }
    catch (ParseException e)
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 3. If !(stop.arrivalTime < stop.departureTime), return false.
    if (!(stop.getArrivalTime().before(stop.getDepartureTime())))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 4. If now >= this.departureTime, return false.
    if (now != null && this.getDepartureTime() != null && !now.before(this.getDepartureTime()))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // 5. If stopovers already contains a stopover at stop.airport, return false.
    for (Stopover existing : this.getStopovers())
    {
      if (existing != null && existing.getAirport() != null && existing.getAirport().equals(stop.getAirport()))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // 6. Append stop to stopovers.
    this.getStopovers().add(stop);

    // 7. Return true.
    return true;
  }

  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.

  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;

  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   *   8. Append the new Reservation to f.reservations.
   *   9. Return true.
```

6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a new Reservation for a single passenger on a given flight, links it to this Booking and the Flight, and sets the reservation's initial status to PENDING.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null or passenger is null/empty, return false.
   *   2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
   *   3. If now >= f.departureTime, return false (cannot reserve after or at departure).
   *   4. If f already has a reservation whose passenger.name equals passenger (case-insensitive trim), return false (no duplicate passenger on the flight).
   *   5. Generate a new unique id for the reservation.
   *   6. Create a new Passenger with name = passenger, a new Reservation with that id, status = PENDING, flight = f, passenger = the new Passenger.
   *   7. Append the new Reservation to this Booking's reservations list.
   *   8. Append the new Reservation to f.reservations.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: reservations
   * Related Classes: Reservation, Passenger, Flight, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - f (Flight) — the open flight the passenger is being reserved on.
   *   - passenger (String) — the passenger's full name; must be unique on the flight.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output: boolean — true if a new PENDING reservation was successfully created and linked; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: f is not null, f is open for booking (openForBooking == true), now is strictly before f.departureTime, passenger is a non-empty string, and no existing reservation on f carries the same passenger name (case-insensitive, trimmed).
   * Post-condition: A new Reservation (status PENDING) is added to both this Booking's reservations and f.reservations, with a newly created Passenger and a unique reservation id; the flight's openForBooking status is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean createReservation(final Flight f, final String passenger, final Date now)
  {
    // Step 1: Validate inputs
    if (f == null || passenger == null || passenger.trim().isEmpty())
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 2: Check flight is open for booking
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 3: Check current time is strictly before departure time
    Date departureTime = f.getDepartureTime();
    if (departureTime == null || now == null || !now.before(departureTime))
    {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 4: Check for duplicate passenger on the flight (case-insensitive trim)
    String trimmedPassenger = passenger.trim();
    for (Reservation existing : f.getReservations())
    {
      Passenger existingPassenger = existing.getPassenger();
      if (existingPassenger != null && existingPassenger.getName() != null
          && existingPassenger.getName().trim().equalsIgnoreCase(trimmedPassenger))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Step 5: Generate a new unique id for the reservation
    String newId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    // Step 6: Create a new Passenger and a new Reservation, link them, and set status
    Passenger newPassenger = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createPassenger();
    newPassenger.setName(trimmedPassenger);

    Reservation newReservation = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createReservation();
    newReservation.setId(newId);
    newReservation.setStatus(ReservationStatus.PENDING);
    newReservation.setFlight(f);
    newReservation.setPassenger(newPassenger);

    // Step 7: Append the new Reservation to this Booking's reservations list
    this.getReservations().add(newReservation);

    // Step 8: Append the new Reservation to f.reservations
    f.getReservations().add(newReservation);

    // Step 9: Return true
    return true;
  }

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a new Booking for this Customer covering a list of passengers on a specific open flight, generating one PENDING Reservation per passenger.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null or listOfPassengerNames is null or empty, return false.
   *   2. If f.openForBooking == false, return false.
   *   3. If now >= f.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. Check that listOfPassengerNames contains no duplicates (case-insensitive, trimmed); if duplicates exist, return false.
   *   5. Create a new Booking b and set b.customer = this.
   *   6. For each name in listOfPassengerNames:
   *        - Call b.createReservation(f, name, now).
   *        - If it returns false, abandon the booking (roll back) and return false.
   *   7. Append b to this Customer's bookings.
   *   8. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings
   * Related Classes: Flight, Booking, Reservation, Passenger, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - f (Flight) — the open flight to be booked.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   *   - listOfPassengerNames (List<String>) — the non-empty list of passenger names to be reserved on the flight; must contain no duplicates.
   * Output: boolean — true if a booking was created and all reservations were added successfully; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: f is an open, not-yet-departed flight; listOfPassengerNames is non-empty and contains no duplicate names; now precedes f.departureTime.
   * Post-condition: A new Booking is created and added to this Customer's bookings; the flight gains a new PENDING Reservation for every supplied passenger name.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model listOfPassengerNamesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean addBooking(final Flight f, final Date now, final EList<String> listOfPassengerNames)
  {
    if (f == null || listOfPassengerNames == null || listOfPassengerNames.isEmpty())
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (now.compareTo(f.getDepartureTime()) >= 0)
    {
      return false;
    }
    Set<String> seen = new HashSet<String>();
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames)
    {
      String key = name == null ? null : name.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!seen.add(key))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    Booking b = FlightsFactory.eINSTANCE.createBooking();
    b.setCustomer(this);
    for (String name : listOfPassengerNames)
    {
      if (!b.createReservation(f, name, now))
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    this.getBookings().add(b);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Confirms an existing PENDING Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.
   *   2. If r.status != PENDING, return false (only PENDING reservations can be confirmed).
   *   3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
   *   5. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings
   * Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to confirm.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully confirmed; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the reservation's status is PENDING; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime.
   * Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CONFIRMED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings())
    {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations())
      {
        if (reservationID != null && reservationID.equals(r.getId()))
        {
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING)
          {
            return false;
          }
          Flight rf = r.getFlight();
          if (rf == null || !rf.isOpenForBooking() || now.compareTo(rf.getDepartureTime()) >= 0)
          {
            return false;
          }
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Cancels an existing Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings())
    {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations())
      {
        if (reservationID != null && reservationID.equals(r.getId()))

          if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CANCELED)
          {
            return false;

          {
            return false;
          }
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;

    return false;

   *   2. If r.status != PENDING, return false (only PENDING reservations can be confirmed).
   *   3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. Set r.status = CONFIRMED.
   *   5. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings
   * Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to confirm.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully confirmed; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the reservation's status is PENDING; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime.
   * Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CONFIRMED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean confirm(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings())
    {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations())
      {
        if (reservationID != null && reservationID.equals(r.getId()))
        {
          if (r.getStatus() != ReservationStatus.PENDING)
          {
            return false;
          }
          Flight rf = r.getFlight();
          if (rf == null || !rf.isOpenForBooking() || now.compareTo(rf.getDepartureTime()) >= 0)
          {
            return false;
          }
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Cancels an existing Reservation belonging to this Customer, provided the underlying flight has not yet departed and is still open for booking.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Search this Customer's bookings for a booking b whose reservations include a reservation r with r.id == reservationID; if none, return false.
   *   2. If r.status == CANCELED, return false (already canceled).
   *   3. If r.flight is null, r.flight.openForBooking == false, or now >= r.flight.departureTime, return false.
   *   4. Set r.status = CANCELED.
   *   5. Return true.
   * Related Features: bookings
   * Related Classes: Booking, Reservation, Flight, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - reservationID (String) — the unique id of the reservation to cancel.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format.
   * Output: boolean — true if the reservation was successfully canceled; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: A reservation with the given reservationID exists in one of this Customer's bookings; the associated flight is still open for booking; now is before that flight's departureTime; the reservation is not already CANCELED.
   * Post-condition: The matching reservation's status is set to CANCELED; the customer's bookings and the flight's reservations remain otherwise unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean cancel(final String reservationID, final Date now)
  {
    for (Booking b : this.getBookings())
    {
      for (Reservation r : b.getReservations())
      {
        if (reservationID != null && reservationID.equals(r.getId()))
        {
          if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CANCELED)
          {
            return false;
          }
          Flight rf = r.getFlight();

          {
            return false;
          }
          r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
          return true;
        }
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Adds a newly created Flight object to the Airline's collection of flights so that the airline can subsequently manage it (e.g., publish or close).
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null, return without action.
   *   2. If f.id already exists in flights, return without action (avoid duplicates).
   *   3. Otherwise, append f to the flights list.
   * Related Features: flights (the list of Flight objects owned by the Airline)
   * Related Classes: Flight
   * Input: f (Flight) — a newly created flight instance to be associated with the airline. Its id must be unique within the airline.
   * Output: void — no return value.
   * Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight with a unique id that is not already present in this Airline's flights list.
   * Post-condition: The given Flight f is stored in this Airline's flights list, making it available for further operations such as publishFlight/closeFlight.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void addFlight(final Flight f)
  {
    if (f == null)
    {
      return;
    }
    for (Flight existing : getFlights())
    {
      if (existing.getId() != null && existing.getId().equals(f.getId()))
      {
        return;
      }
    }
    getFlights().add(f);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Removes a Flight from the Airline's collection of flights (typically used for administrative cleanup or after a flight has been added in error).
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null, return without action.
   *   2. Remove f from the flights list if present.
   *   3. Return without action otherwise.
   * Related Features: flights
   * Related Classes: Flight
   * Input: f (Flight) — the flight to be removed from the airline's catalog.
   * Output: void — no return value.
   * Pre-condition: f is a non-null Flight reference; it may or may not currently exist in the airline's flights list.
   * Post-condition: If f was in flights, it is no longer present; otherwise the list is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT

  public void removeFlight(final Flight f)
  {
    if (f == null)
    {
      return;
    }
    getFlights().remove(f);
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Publishes a newly created Flight to the public booking catalog after validating its timestamps, temporal ordering, and route integrity.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If f is null, return false.
   *   2. Validate that f.departureTime and f.arrivalTime are parseable timestamps in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format; if not, return false.
   *   3. If f.departureAirport == null or f.arrivalAirport == null, return false.
   *   4. If f.departureAirport.id == f.arrivalAirport.id, return false (route integrity).
   *   5. If !(now < f.departureTime < f.arrivalTime), return false (temporal consistency).
   *   6. If f.openForBooking == true, return false (a flight can only be published once).
   *   7. Set f.openForBooking = true.
   *   8. If f is not yet in flights, add it to flights.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights
   * Related Classes: Flight, Airport
   * Input:
   *   - f (Flight) — the flight to be published; contains id, departure/arrival Airports, departureTime, arrivalTime, and openForBooking status.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used as the lower bound for departure time.
   * Output: boolean — true if the flight is successfully published; false if any validation fails or it has already been published.
   * Pre-condition: f is a non-null, freshly created Flight with valid timestamps, distinct departure and arrival airports, f.openForBooking == false, and now precedes f.departureTime.
   * Post-condition: f.openForBooking becomes true; the flight is included in this Airline's flights list and becomes available for customer bookings.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean publishFlight(final Flight f, final Date now)
  {
    if (f == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    sdf.setLenient(false);
    Date departure = null;
    Date arrival = null;
    try
    {
      if (f.getDepartureTime() != null)
      {
        departure = sdf.parse(sdf.format(f.getDepartureTime()));
      }
      if (f.getArrivalTime() != null)
      {
        arrival = sdf.parse(sdf.format(f.getArrivalTime()));
      }
    }
    catch (ParseException e)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (departure == null || arrival == null)
    {
      return false;
    }

    if (f.getDepartureAirport() == null || f.getArrivalAirport() == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (f.getDepartureAirport().getId() != null && f.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(f.getArrivalAirport().getId()))
    {
      return false;
    }

    Date nowParsed = null;
    try
    {
      nowParsed = sdf.parse(sdf.format(now));
    }
    catch (ParseException e)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!(nowParsed.before(departure) && departure.before(arrival)))
    {
      return false;
    }

    if (f.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }

    f.setOpenForBooking(true);
    if (!getFlights().contains(f))
    {
      getFlights().add(f);
    }
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Closes an existing open flight that has not yet departed, marking it as no longer bookable and canceling all of its currently CONFIRMED reservations.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Find the Flight in flights whose id equals flightId; if not found, return false.
   *   2. If flight.openForBooking == false, return false (only an open flight can be closed).
   *   3. Compute the calendar day of now and the calendar day of flight.departureTime.
   *   4. If these two calendar days are equal, return false (closing is forbidden on the same calendar day as departure).
   *   5. If now >= flight.departureTime, return false (the flight has already departed).
   *   6. Set flight.openForBooking = false.
   *   7. For each reservation r in flight.reservations, if r.status == CONFIRMED, set r.status = CANCELED.
   *   8. Return true.
   * Related Features: flights
   * Related Classes: Flight, Reservation, ReservationStatus
   * Input:
   *   - flightId (String) — the unique id of the target flight.
   *   - now (EDate) — the current timestamp in yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss format, used for temporal checks.
   * Output: boolean — true if the flight was successfully closed and all confirmed reservations were canceled; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: A flight with the given flightId exists; now is the current timestamp; the flight is currently openForBooking; the current calendar day differs from the flight's departure calendar day.
   * Post-condition: flight.openForBooking becomes false; every previously CONFIRMED reservation of that flight transitions to CANCELED; PENDING reservations are left untouched.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean closeFlight(final String flightId, final Date now)
  {
    Flight target = null;
    for (Flight f : getFlights())
    {
      if (f.getId() != null && f.getId().equals(flightId))
      {
        target = f;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (target == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!target.isOpenForBooking())
    {
      return false;
    }
    Calendar calNow = Calendar.getInstance();
    calNow.setTime(now);
    Calendar calDep = Calendar.getInstance();
    calDep.setTime(target.getDepartureTime());
    if (calNow.get(Calendar.YEAR) == calDep.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
        calNow.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == calDep.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR))
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (!now.before(target.getDepartureTime()))
    {
      return false;
    }
    target.setOpenForBooking(false);
    for (Reservation r : target.getReservations())
    {
      if (r.getStatus() == ReservationStatus.CONFIRMED)
      {
        r.setStatus(ReservationStatus.CANCELED);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Searches the Airline's flights for those that depart from a given origin airport, arrive at a given destination airport, and depart on a given date.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Initialize an empty result list.
   *   2. For each Flight f in flights:
   *        - if f.departureAirport != null and f.departureAirport.id == origin,
   *          and f.arrivalAirport != null and f.arrivalAirport.id == dest,
   *          and the date portion of f.departureTime equals the date portion of date,
   *          then add f to result.
   *   3. Return result (empty list if no matches).
   * Related Features: flights
   * Related Classes: Flight, Airport
   * Input:
   *   - origin (String) — the id of the desired departure Airport.
   *   - dest (String) — the id of the desired arrival Airport.
   *   - date (EDate) — the desired departure date; the time portion may be ignored when matching.
   * Output: List<Flight> — the list of matching flights; may be empty.
   * Pre-condition: None. The search is read-only and does not modify the airline's state.
   * Post-condition: A filtered list of flights is returned; the airline's flights list is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public EList<Flight> searchFlights(final String origin, final String dest, final Date date)
  {
    BasicEList<Flight> result = new BasicEList<Flight>();
    Calendar calDate = Calendar.getInstance();
    calDate.setTime(date);
    for (Flight f : getFlights())
    {
      if (f.getDepartureAirport() == null || f.getArrivalAirport() == null)
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (f.getDepartureAirport().getId() == null || !f.getDepartureAirport().getId().equals(origin))
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (f.getArrivalAirport().getId() == null || !f.getArrivalAirport().getId().equals(dest))
      {
        continue;
      }
      if (f.getDepartureTime() == null)
      {
        continue;
      }
      Calendar calDep = Calendar.getInstance();
      calDep.setTime(f.getDepartureTime());
      if (calDep.get(Calendar.YEAR) == calDate.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
          calDep.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == calDate.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR))
      {
        result.add(f);
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
